   79. cēvaka murārikaḷ purārikaḷ terittaṉa
         civākama vitanterivarē
   pūvakam viṭātavarka ḷōtavuṭa ṉōtuvar
         parampara purantararumē.
   e-tu: tēvi tirukkōyiliṭattu viṭṭuṇukkaḷ palarumiruntu makātēvarālaruḷic
   ceyyappaṭṭa civākamamāṉa caivacittāntap parappukkaḷai ārāyvar; aṉṟiyē
   ivvākamaṅkaḷaiyē piramākkaḷōta avaruṭaṉē vaḻivaḻip paramacuvāmiyintirar
   ōtuvar. e-ṟu.
   murārikaḷ - muraṉeṉpāṉōracuraṉaik koṉṟaruḷiṉa makā viṭṭuṇukkaḷ. purāri
   - tiripuraṅkaḷai erittaruḷiṉa makātēvar. murārikaḷeṉpatu paṉmaivācakam.
   purārikaḷeṉpatu mutaṉmaivācakam, palar īcuvarariṉmaiyāl. pūvakam
   viṭātavar piramākkaḷ; avarkaḷōta veṉpataṉaik kaṟpikkaveṉṟu koḷḷalumām.
   vēṟu.

   83. naccukkaṇ mulaimē liṭutukiṟku nakupoṟ

   86. aṅṅkaṇ muḷarimala raṉmaiyatu tiṅkaḷaṟiyat

   87. aṭaiya mōkiṉika ḷāyiṉarko lavvuruniṉain

   e-tu: paramēcuvari tirukkōyililirukkum yōkiṉippeṇkaḷ ellārum iyalpākavē

   88. cūḻu miṉṉoḷi nivantucura nāṭiyarkaḷun

   tamiḻāṟcitaintu [*] cemmārāyiṟṟu.

   89. tarava rumpuṭavi kuṟaiyavuḷar cākiṉikaḷē
   parava runtakaiya nāyakipa tākiṉikaḷē.
   e-tu: piramā paṭaikka vantu tōṉṟum pūmaṇṭalamaṭaṅka iṭaṅkuṟaiyumpaṭi
   uḷarāvārkaḷ cākiṉikaḷeṉpār, oruvarālum pukaḻutaṟkariya aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya
   nāyakiyuṭaiya koṭippaṭaikaḷ. e-ṟu.
   ippāṭṭait tāvaruveṉavum pāvaruveṉavum neṭṭeḻuttiṟ ṟākkik kūviḷameṉṟu
   pāṭamōtiṟ keṭutaṟkariyaveṉavum parantuvaru maḻakeṉavuṅ koḷka. patāciṉi
   - koṭippaṭai. cākiṉikaḷāvār, īcuvaraṉatukātalāṟ pirāmaṇikaḷ vayiṟṟiṟ
   piṟanta naṅkaimāreṉa vuṇarka.
   --------
   90. nakuna kuṅkaṭavu ḷittakaiya mātarnakaipōy
   [+]mokumo keṉṟakila lōkamu muḻaṅkuvaṉavē.
   e-tu: yōkiṉikaḷ carmiṉikaḷ cākiṉikaḷ mutalāya ippeṟṟiyōrāṉa ellāt
   teyvappeṇkaḷum paramēcuvari tirukkōyilil tirumuṟṟattiruntu cirittu
   viḷaiyāṭuñ cirippup pōy mokumokeṉṉum aṉukaraṇa makācattampiṟantu
   caturttacapuvaṉamu muḻaṅkum. e-ṟu.
   nakunakaipōyeṉṟu koḷka. mokumokeṉpatu iraṭṭaik kiḷavi; [#] "iraṭṭaik
   kiḷavi yiraṭṭiṟ pirinticaiyā". eṉṉai? "vāḷamar nīntum pōḻtu vaḻuvaḻut
   toḻiyu maṉṟē" ((257) 257) itu cintāmaṇi; "kuṟukuṟu naṭantu ciṟukai
   nīṭṭi" ((188) 188.) itu puṟam.
   -----
   [+] "mokumo keṉṟoli mikuntamaru kaṅkaḷpalavē" kaliṅka. (100) 100.
   [#] tol. kiḷavi. cū. (48) 48.
   -----
   91. [*]talaiya rintuviṭu vāruyir viṭārtalaivimuṉ
   ṉilaiya runtamatu [+]meyyeriyi ṉiṉṟerivarē.
   e-tu: paramēcuvariyuṭaiya yāmaḷa cāttira mantiracātakar, tammuṭaiya
   talaiyait tāmēyarintarintu talaiyaiviṭṭu uṭalaippaṟṟiniṟpār, tam
   pirāṇaṉkaḷaiviṭār; paramēcuvarimuṉṉē piṟarukku niṟkavariya
   nilaiyilēniṉṟu tammuṭaiya carīraṅkaḷiluṇṭākiya akkiṉiyiṉālē niṉṟerivar.
   e-ṟu.
   eṉavē talaiyai aṟuttuviṭṭu uṭalōṭēniṉṟu taṅkaḷ yōkāṅkattāṟ pirāṇaṉaip
   pōkāmaṟṟaṭuttu nāpīkamalattu akkiṉi maṇṭalattai yeḻuppi
   irutayakamalattiṟ ṟīpattiṟkoḷutti atuvēneruppāka niṉṟu
   yōkākkiṉiyamaippareṉṟavāṟu.
   ---
   [*] "mōṭi muṉṟalaiyai vaippa rēmuṭi kulainta kuñciyai muṭipparē, yāṭi
   niṉṟukuru tippu tuttilaka mammu kattiṉi lamaipparē" (cilap. (5: 76 -
   88) 5 : 76 - 88, mēṟ); "aṭikkaḻutti ṉoṭuñcirattai yarivarālō
   varintacira maṇaṅkiṉkaik koṭupparālō, koṭuttaciraṅ koṟṟavaiyaip
   paravumālō kuṟaiyuṭaluṅ kumpiṭṭu niṟkumālō", " kaṭaṉamaintatu
   karuntalai yarintapoḻutē kaṭava toṉṟumilai yeṉṟuviḷai yāṭumuṭalē"
   (kaliṅka. (97, 99) 97, 99); "aṭalaruntiṟa ṉiṉaivuṭaṉ cilararipacuntalai
   palikoṭut, tuṭalvalañceya varamiruntavai yoṉṟiraṇṭeṉa veṇṇumē."
   iraṇiya. (94) 94.
   [+] "meyviḷakkiṉār mēvukōyiluk kevviḷakkuvē ṟiṉiviḷakkumē"
   aññavataipparaṇi, (87) 87.
   ----
   vēṟu.
   92. akavaṉaca mukavaṉaca mavaimalara varivār
   nakavaṉaca malarkuviya valamvaruvar nakarē.
   e-tu: irutayakamalamaṟukkum poḻutu vataṉakamalamalaravum mukakamalamāṉa
   tamatu talaiyaiyaṟukkum poḻutu irutayakamalamalaravum iraṇṭum iraṇṭu
   kūṟiṭa aṟukkum poḻutu iraṇṭumalarai arivārāy arintu koṇṭu cīruṭaiya
   kaittāmaraimalarkuvittuk kumpiṭṭut tēvi kōṭṭattaip
   pirataṭcaṇamākavaruvar, cilamantiracātakar. e-ṟu.
   vaṉacam - tāmarai. nakavaṉacam - malarnta vaṉacam; aṉṟi ukiruṭaiya
   vaṉacameṉṟumām. nakareṉpatu kōvil.
   ------
   vēṟu.
   93. kokko ḻuṅkupaṭa vōṭimukil kūṭiyaṉaiyā
   rakko ḻuṅkupaṭu kañcuka malampavuḷarē.
   e-tu: ākācattu varuṣākālattup paṟantu tiriyum kokkeṉṉum peyaraiyuṭaiya
   veṇpaṟavai niraipaṭa ōṭivarak karumukil ataṟkumēlākak kūṭiṉavai
   pōṉṟirukkumpaṭi caṅkumaṇivaṭam kōkkappaṭṭa karuñcaṭṭaiyaik
   kalantiṭṭavar palaruḷar. e-ṟu.
   oḻuṅku - nirai. akkoḻuṅku - caṅkumaṇivaṭam. paṭutal - uṇṭātal.
   alamputal - kalattal; acaitaṟkum peyar; [*]vēlaivaṭimpalampaniṉṟāṉeṉṉuñ
   collāluṇarka.
   -----
   [*] vēlaivaṭimpalampaniṉṟāṉ - ukkirakumārapāṇṭiyaṉ; "mālaimuṭi yōṉvēlai
   vaṭimpalampa niṉṟataṉāl, vēlaivaṭim palampaniṉṟā ṉeṉṟeṅkum
   viḷaṅkiyatāl" (tiruvāla. (21: 7) 21: 7); “āvu māṉiyaṟ pārppaṉa "
   (puṟanā. (9) 9) eṉṉumpāṭṭiṉ (10) 10-vatu aṭiyiṉ kīḻkkuṟippaip pārkka.
   ----
   94. inti rātiyaru mekkamala yōṉikaḷumē
   canti rātiyaru mattalaivi cātakarkaḷē.
   e-tu: paramēcuvari tirukkōyilil mantiracātakam paṇṇuvārai
   eṇṇakkaṭavatō! intiraṉ akkiṉi varuṇaṉ vāyuvātitēvarkaḷum
   ellālōkaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa piramākkaḷum eṅkumuḷḷa cantirātittiya
   naṭcattirakaṇaṅkaḷellām paramēcuvariyuṭaiya kōyililiruntu
   mantiracātakam paṇṇuvārkaḷ. e-ṟu.
   itaṟkuppayaṉ, avarkku aicuvariyaṅkaḷaṟātē cōtiyum kīrttiyu muṇṭātaleṉa
   vuṇarka.
   vēṟu.
   95. aṭaiyāḷa muḷarit talaiviyāti maṭavā
   ruṭaiyā ṭiruvakam paṭiyilyō kiṉikaḷē.
   e-tu: tāmarai mutalāṉa aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyarākiya srītēvi mutalāṉa
   piratāṉappeṇkaḷellārum paramēcuvariyuṭaiya tirukkōyiṟ [+]
   ṟiruvakampaṭip peṇṭukaḷil yōkaniṭṭai paṇṇiṉa yōkiṉip peṇkaḷ. e-ṟu.
   caracuvatiyaiyuṅkoḷka, avaḷum nittiyacittippeṇṇātaliṉ. aṭaiyāṇ
   muḷariyeṉṉumpāṭamaḻakitu; aṭai - ilai.
   -----
   [+] tiruvakampaṭippeṇṭukaḷ - tiruvaṇukkattoṇṭu ceyyum makaḷir; akampaṭi
   - uḷḷiṭam.
   -----
   vēṟu.
   96. cuḻalvaṭṭat [*] tuṭikoṭṭat tuṉainaṭṭat tiṉarē
   taḻalvaṭṭat taṉineṟṟit taṟukaṭkoṭ piṉarē.
   e-tu: cuḻaṉṟirukkum vaṭṭattaiyuṭaiya tuṭiyeṉṉum paṟai koṭṭumaḷavil
   viraintāṭuṅ kūttiṉar ....... e-ṟu.
   tuṉai - viraivu. koṭpu – [+] cuḻaṟṟi.
   ----
   [*] "tamarukaṅkaṭaru kiṉṟacati yiṉkaṇvaruvār" kaliṅka. (101) 101.
   [+] cuḻaṟṟi - cuḻaṟci; kaḻaṟṟipōla.
   -----
   97. kaḻuvaippuk kaṟaveṭṭik kavarcuṟṟat tiṉarē
   yuḻuvaicciṟ ṟurivaippac cutirappaṭ ṭiṉarē.
   e-tu: uyirkkaḻumutalāṉa kaḻukkaḷiṟpukku avaṟṟai aṟumpaṭi veṭṭip
   piṇaṅkaḷaikkoḷḷum pēyccuṟṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyar cilar; pulikaḷuṭaiya
   ciṟutōlaiyurittu attōlai vāṅki utirappacumaikeṭāmē yuṭutta
   uṭaippaṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiyar cilar. e-ṟu.
   uḻuvaiyutirappaṭṭeṉka; uḻuvai - puli; urivai - tōl; "ariyi ṉurivai
   mēkalai yāṭṭi" ((12 : 62) 12 : 62) eṉpatu cilappatikāram.
   paccutirappaṭṭeṉpataṉ payaṉ, vilaikkuk koṇṭu uṭutta tōlalla; tāṅkaḷē
   puliyaippiṭittuk kollātē urittatōleṉavuṇarka.
   -----------
   98. akaimattat taḷivarkkat taḷakakkot tiṉarē
   pakaikuttip payilcattip parumukkap piṉarē.
   e-tu: malarnta [#] matamattaiyiṟ pūviṉvaṇṭiṉañ cēravaṇintu vaitta
   aḷakakkottukkaḷai yuṭaiyarāyum pakaikaḷaikkuttip paḻakiya vēlāṉa mūṉṟu
   kavaṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya āyutattai yuṭaiyarāyumiruppar cilar. e-ṟu.
   akaittal - valiyamalarttalumām. aḷi - vaṇṭu. mukkappu - cūlam.
   ---
   [#] matamattai - ūmattai; itu matamattameṉavum vaḻaṅkum; "kaḷḷārnta
   pūṅkoṉṟai matamattaṅkatirmatiyam, uḷḷārnta caṭaimuṭi yemperumāṉ"
   tēvāram.
   ----
   99. ukunaccut talaineṭṭeṭ ṭurakakkac ciṉarē
   takuputtap putumakkaṭ ṭalaimakkaṭ ṭiṉarē.
   e-tu: viṣatārai ōvātu coritarum talaikaḷaiyuṭaiyavāy neṭiyavāyirukkum
   eṭṭuppāmpukaḷaiyē kaccāka araikkumēṟ kaṭṭiṉa kaṭṭaiyuṭaiyar cilar.
   ataṟkut takkavākac cērtta maṉitar talaikaḷai avaiyiṟṟiṉmēlē
   maṇivaṭamākak kaṭṭiṉa kaṭṭaiyuṭaiyar cilar. e-ṟu.
   eṭṭurakamāvaṉa : anantaṉ, vācuki, taṭcakaṉ, kārkkōṭakaṉ, caṅkaṉ,
   kuḷikaṉ, paṟpaṉ, makāpaṟpaṉeṉavuṇarka. ivveṭṭum eṇmar kaṭṭiṉa valla;
   ivviṉaṅkaḷil terintu palaruṅkaṭṭiṉa veṉavuṇarka. makkaṭṭu, maṇikkaṭṭāṉa
   muṉkaikkum kalaiyiṟ kaṭṭiṉa kaṭṭukkum peyar. makkaṭṭalaiyeṉpatiṉ
   muṭivuṇarka. takumeṉṟa collāl, nākarācākkaḷukkut takkapaṭiyuḷḷa
   maṉuṣyarācākkaḷ talaiyeṉavuṇarka. itu nilaiyāmaipporuḷ.
   ---------
   100. kuṭaraṭṭat torucekkark [*]kurutippoṭ ṭiṉarē
   paṭarpoṟkaic ceṟiyakkuc caripappat tiṉarē.
   e-tu: vīrapuruṣar kuṭarkaḷilutirattai oruviralāṟṟōṭṭu
   neṟṟiyiliṭṭukkoṇṭa poṭṭuṭaiyar cilar; mikappaṭarnta aḻakiya
   kaikaḷiṟceṟinta caṅkumaṇiccari pattuppattiṉaiyuṭaiyar cilar. e-ṟu.
   vīrapuruṣar añcātupaṭṭāl avarvayiṟṟilutirattai oruviralāṟ ṟoṭṭu
   neṟṟiyiliṭuvareṉṟuṇarka.
   mikappaṭarnta aḻakiya kaiyeṉpatu [+] pēykkaḻaku; itu [#]
   kōmāḷameṉavuṇarka.
   ----
   [*] tilakamiṭ ṭaḻakuṟat tirivarpai ravarkaḷē ", " kurutippoṭṭu nutal."
   iraṇiyavataipparaṇi, (100, 111) 100, 111.
   [+] "pukalva teṉkolpēyk kaḻakupol lāmaiyē yaṉṟō" eṉpatu
   mōkavataipparaṇi, (389) 389.
   [#] "kōmāḷa māṉa kuṟikkaḻuttikaḷ." tiruppukaḻ, virālimalai.
   ---
   101. irupakkat torupakkat teṟivacrat tiṉarē
   yorupakkat toḷivaṭṭat torupoṟṟaṭ ṭiṉarē.
   e-tu: valamum iṭamumāṉa iraṇṭupakkattilum valappakkamāṉa orupakkattiṟ
   catturukkaḷai eṟiyāniṉṟa vaccirāyutattaiyuṭaiyar, maṟṟai orupakkamāṉa
   iṭakkaiyilē cōtiviṭum poṉṉāṟcamaitta [$] vaṭṭaṇaik kiṭukaiyuṭaiyar
   cilar. e-ṟu.
   vacciram - vaccirāyutam.
   ---
   [$] vaṭṭaṇaikkiṭuku - vaṭṭamākiya kēṭakam; "kaṭṭeḻil cērnta vaṭṭaṇaip
   palakai." peruṅ. (1. 42 : 63) 1.42.63.
   ----
   102. taḻaivarkkak karuvepput taṭicakrat tiṉarē
   kaḻaimuttup potikakkak kiḻikaṭkaṭ ciyarē.
   e-tu: pacuntaḻaiyaik karukumpaṭi ceykiṉṟa veppāṉa uṣṇa viyātiyaik
   keṭukkuñ cakkarapūcaiyaiyuṭaiyar cilar; avar, mūṅkilkaḷ muttuppotiyaip
   puṟappaṭaviṭumpaṭi kiḻikkiṉṟa kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭiṉar. e-ṟu.
   "cakkarapūcai pūcippār" eṉpatu pairavattiṟpāṭṭu. kiḻikaṇṇeṉṟatu
   mūṅkiṟkaṇṇiṉ kiḻivai. kaṭci - kāṭu; "kaṟaiyaṇaṟ kuṟumpūḻ kaṭcic cēkkum"
   ((205) 205.) itu perumpāṇāṟu.
   3. kāṭupāṭiyatu muṟṟiṟṟu.
